=== MD 11 ===
## Why this composition survived

The opening route already wins a broad public-source ladder, so V19 changes only the part with reliable observation-driven leverage. RC5 repairs realized weeds. Terminal-8 monetizes actual endgame state. Clone-gated forecasting infers one-to-six-step market timing against visibly nearby route families, while multi-step debt repayment prevents the counter from silently deleting core actions.
